# splat claim list: <target function index> <our symbol> per line (# starts a comment).
# A claimed function is assembled from the body the plain skeleton link produces for that symbol, re-pointed into
# the target (make -C src splat); the build fails while any claim no longer re-points into exactly the target's body.
# make -C src splat-claim appends new matches from the scan and keeps every existing line.
